For each j in [0,n-1], c_k[j]= a_{k-j} (if 0≤k-j<n) + a_{j-k} (if 0≤j-k<n).
First term T1[j] = ∑_{k=j}^{j+n-1} sw[k] a[k-j], but k max 2n-2, so min(j+n-1,2n-2). For j≤n-1, j+n-1 ≤2n-2? If j=n-1, 2n-2 yes. So k j..j+n-1. T1[j]=∑_{m=0}^{n-1} a[m] sw[j+m]. This is earlier c1.
Second term T2[j] = ∑_{k=j-n+1}^{j} sw[k] a[j-k], but k min 0. For j<n, lower negative, so k=0..j. In terms m=j-k from 0..j: ∑_{m=0}^j a[m] sw[j-m]. But note for m up to n-1; here j. Alternatively T2[j]=∑_{m=0}^{n-1} a[m] sw[j-m] with valid k=j-m ≥0 => m≤j. So T2[j] = sliding dot product over prefix.
We can compute both via FFT full convolution.
T1[j] = (a * sw_rev)[2n-2-j] as before (for a length n, sw length 2n-1). Let conv1 = convolve(a, sw[::-1]). Then T1[j]=conv1[2n-2-j]. Need verify for j=0: T1[0]=∑_{m=0}^{n-1}a[m] sw[m]. conv1[p]=∑ a[m] sw_rev[p-m]=∑a[m] sw[2n-2-(p-m)]=∑a[m] sw[2n-2-p+m]. Set =sw[m] =>2n-2-p+m=m =>p=2n-2. OK.
T2[j] = ∑_{m=0}^{j} a[m] sw[j-m] = (a * sw)[j] (linear convolution) for j=0..n-1. Because convolution a*sw at p=j: ∑_{m=0}^{j} a[m] sw[j-m] (since a index beyond length0). So T2 = conv2[:n] where conv2=convolve(a, sw).
Thus C[j]=T1[j]+T2[j]. Nice.
Gradient = 2n * a * (C - 2*sw_dot_b). Check factor: f=2n lse, ∂lse/∂θ = ∑ sw δb/θ. δb/θ=a_j(c_k-2b_k). Sum gives C -2bsum. So grad=2n a (C-2Σswb). Good.

C = T1+T2.

Pseudo:
```
def _softmax(theta):
    th = theta - np.max(theta)
    e = np.exp(th)
    return e/np.sum(e)

def _conv_square(a):
    n=len(a)
    size=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    A=fft(a,size)
    return np.real(ifft(A*A,size)[:2*n-1])

def _lse_value(theta, tau):
    a=_softmax(theta); b=_conv_square(a); bm=max(b); s=(b-bm)/tau; es=exp(s); return 2*n*(bm+tau*log(sum(es)))

def _lse_grad(theta, tau):
    a=_softmax(theta); n=len(a); b=_conv_square(a)
    bm=max(b); s=(b-bm)/tau; es=exp(s); sw=es/sum(es)
    sw_dot_b=np.dot(sw,b)
    # b has length 2n-1
    size=next_fast_len(3*n)
    A=fft(a,size)
    SW=fft(sw,size)
    conv2=np.real(ifft(A*SW,size))  # length 3n-2, need first n
    T2=conv2[:n]
    SWrev=fft(sw[::-1],size)
    conv1=np.real(ifft(A*SWrev,size))
    T1=conv1[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    C=T1+T2
    return 2.0*n*a*(C - 2.0*sw_dot_b)
```

Now, initial starts. Let's define a function `_random_theta(rng,n,sd)`. For deterministic reproducible, seed.

Heuristic starts:
- Uniform zeros.
- Linear trend: t = np.linspace(0,1,n); theta = slope*(t-0.5). Slopes [0.5,1,2,-...]. For softmax, large slope exp may create near exponential; may be bad but okay.
- Quadratic: theta = a*(t-0.5)^2 + b*t.
- Sine phases: for k=1..5, theta = amplitude * np.sin(2πk t + phase) or cos. Amplitude 1-3.
- Chirp: theta = amplitude * np.sin(2π c * t^2) for c in [0.5,1,2,3]. This can create low autocorrelation.
- "Barker-like" random ±1: theta = amplitude * signs (sum zero). Maybe.

We can't know which works. Use multiple.

Time allocation: For each n, maybe 5-10 starts. For n=256, fast. For n=4806, maybe only few starts with low K.

We can also do "mutation annealing": keep best theta for each n, periodically add noise and continue. But L-BFGS already.

    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(0, budget_s-10)

    except: parent=None

        update best

       # budget: if n==n_parent and n>512: 600s? else based

def _theta_from_coeffs(z, n, K):
    idx=np.arange(n)
    theta=np.zeros(n)
    for idx in range(K):
       k=idx+1
       theta += z[2*idx]*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*idx/n)
       theta += z[2*idx+1]*np.sin(...)
    # maybe remove mean to avoid scale issues
    theta -= np.mean(theta)
    return theta

def _coeff_value(z, n, K, tau):
   theta=_theta_from_coeffs(...)
   return _lse_value(theta,tau)

def _coeff_grad(z, n, K, tau):
   theta=...
   gtheta=_lse_grad(theta,tau)
   grad=np.zeros(len(z))
   # compute cos/sin derivatives; can compute in loop K
   for k...
       c=np.cos(...); s=np.sin(...)
       grad[2k]=np.dot(gtheta,c); grad[2k+1]=np.dot(gtheta,s)
   return grad
```

Need final code no lambdas. `bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n` is okay but creates list of tuples. `linprog` requires c vector. We can implement `_lp_direction(a, M)` returning g or None.
```
def _lp_direction(a, M):
    n=len(a)
    # if n>1024 return None
    L=2*n-1
    A=np.zeros((L,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        vals=a[:n]  # a[0..n-1]
        A[j:j+n, j] = vals
    c=-np.ones(n)
    b_ub=np.full(L, M)
    res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'time_limit': 30})
    if res.status in (0,3): return res.x
    return None
```
HIGS time_limit? `linprog` options can include `time_limit`. Might not in older? Use try.
But A construction loop O(n^2) and memory. For n=512, 1023x512=525k; fine. Column j has a[:n] in rows j..j+n-1. Check convolution: c[k]=∑_{j} a[k-j]g[j]. For column j, k from j to j+n-1, value a[k-j]=a[0..n-1]. Good.
Feasibility: uniform g=1/n gives A g? For column j vector a, uniform sum (1/n)*sum_j A[:,j]=? Row k sum of a over valid =1, so A (1/n ones)=1/n = M? If M current max maybe >=1/n? For normalized probability, max b can be <1/n if score<2; then uniform g sum1 not feasible because row sums=1 >M? Wait constraints A g ≤M. If M<1/n, uniform g=1/n gives row=1/n >M infeasible. But LP feasible with g=0. The objective max sum maybe less. The state-of-art LP used M = ||f*f||∞; for f normalized sum sqrt(2n), M? They probably set M as current peak of f*f (not cross? f sum sqrt(2n), f*f peak = normalized? For uniform, f*f peak=1? Let's check if sum f=√(2n), a_i=1/√(2n)? Then f*f peak =1? If M<1/n, uniform not feasible but zero feasible; optimum may be small. After normalizing g to sum1, cross-correlation peak = M/sum_g. If sum_g could be <1, not improving directly. But iteration.

Starts generation details:
```
def _generate_starts(n, rng, max_starts=12):
    starts=[]
    starts.append(np.zeros(n))
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    # slopes
    for s in [0.5,1,2,4,-0.5,-1,-2,-4]:
        starts.append(s*(t-0.5))
    # quadratics
    for s in [1,2,4]: starts.append(s*((t-0.5)**2 - (t-0.5)**2?))
    # Fourier deterministic
    for k in [1,2,3,4]:
        for amp in [1,2,4]:
             starts.append(amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*k*t))
             starts.append(amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*t))
    # chirp
    for c in [0.5,1,2,3]:
        starts.append(2*np.sin(2*np.pi*c*t*t))
    # random
    for sd in [0.5,1,2]:
        starts.append(rng.standard_normal(n)*sd)
    # random signs
    u=np.sign(rng.standard_normal(n)); u-=u.mean(); starts.append(2*u)
    return starts[:max_starts]
```
That's many starts (30+). For each n, we cannot run all with full anneal. We can select based on n/time. Maybe generate 12 starts. Use deterministic indices.

Let's test conceptually LSE optimization from uniform for n=256. Uniform is stationary for exact max but finite τ may create gradient due edges, moving to non-symmetric sequence. Let's try to find a parametric sequence mathematically that achieves <1.5. Use probability distribution f with f=1+α m where m is antisymmetric to reduce center but controls tail. The convolution tail peak can be managed by choosing m that is zero near one endpoint so both align? For m=sin(kπx) antisymmetric when k even, high values near middle and endpoints? Let's analyze using Fourier of one-sided convolution. Non-symmetric f has both g(t≤1) and g(t≥1). To avoid tail peaks, one side of f should be low where the other shifted high aligns. This suggests a distribution with support over [0,1] but value profile not symmetric: maybe high at one end and zero at the other, with oscillations. Let's attempt a simple monotone decreasing probability a_i ∝ r^i (geometric). We earlier miscalculated with continuous but let's compute score for geometric discrete length n. It might be below 2? Let's derive exact for geometric a_i = c λ^i (i=0..n-1), sum1. b_k = c^2 λ^k * (min(k+1, 2n-1-k, ?)) for geometric? For full finite geometric: b_k = c^2 λ^k * (n - |?|) not exactly? Since a_i a_{k-i}=c^2 λ^{k}; for k≤n-1, number of i from0..k =k+1, so b_k=c^2 (k+1)λ^k. For k≥n-1, valid i from k-n+1..n-1 count=2n-1-k, and exponent sum i+k-i=k, so b_k=c^2 (2n-1-k)λ^k. Thus b_k = c^2 w_k λ^k. Normalization c=(1-λ^n)/(1-λ)? Since sum c∑λ^i=1 => c=(1-λ)/(1-λ^n).

Helpers:
```
def _safe_softmax(theta):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    tmax = np.max(theta)
    if not np.isfinite(tmax): return np.ones(len(theta))/len(theta)
    e = np.exp(theta - tmax)
    s = np.sum(e)
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.ones(len(theta))/len(theta)
    return e/s
```

`_conv_square(a)`:
```
n=len(a); size=next_fast_len(2*n-1); A=fft(a,size); return np.real(ifft(A*A,size)[:2*n-1])
```

b=_conv_square(an)
b=np.maximum(b,0)
M=np.max(b)
if not finite: return inf
return float(2*len(an)*M)
```

if se<=0: return 1e300

return val if finite else 1e300

def _lse_grad(theta,tau):
  tau=...
  a=_safe_softmax(theta); n=len(a); b=_conv_square(a)
  bm=max(b); s=(b-bm)/tau; es=np.exp(s); se=sum(es)
  sw=es/se
  bclip=np.maximum(b,0)? Use b as is for swb? b may small neg; use b_max maybe. swb=np.dot(sw,b)
  size=next_fast_len(3*n-1)
  A=fft(a,size)
  SWR=fft(sw[::-1], size)
  conv=np.real(ifft(A*SWR,size))
  if conv.size < 2*n-1: T1 = np.zeros(n) ... else T1=conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
  grad=4.0*n*a*(T1 - swb)
  # remove mean component (numerical)
  grad = grad - np.sum(grad)
  # since softmax invariance, sum should zero; subtract mean? But gradient w.r.t theta with invariant should have sum zero, subtracting mean corresponds projection in θ space (adding constant doesn't change a). This is okay and can improve L-BFGS. However if we subtract mean, it's not exact gradient but a valid projected gradient. Do it.
  if not finite all: return zeros
  return grad
```
Wait if we subtract mean of grad, is it still gradient on quotient space? Adding constant to theta leaves a unchanged, true gradient should sum zero, but numerical sum ~0. Subtracting mean is okay. But L-BFGS assumes exact? Fine.

Coefficient basis functions: To avoid large theta values, we can scale.
```
def _theta_from_coeffs(z, n, K):
    theta=np.zeros(n)
    if K<=0: return theta
    idx=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    for k in range(1,K+1):
       ph=2*np.pi*k*idx/n
       theta += z[2*(k-1)]*np.cos(ph)
       theta += z[2*(k-1)+1]*np.sin(ph)
    # subtract mean to keep softmax stable
    theta -= np.mean(theta)
    return theta
```
`_coeff_value(z,n,K,tau)`: call.
`_coeff_grad`: compute gtheta, then grad dot basis. For speed, loop k and arrays. Use `np.dot(gtheta, np.cos(ph))`.

Start generation:

def _theta_starts(n, rng, max_starts=14):
    starts=[]
    starts.append(np.zeros(n))
    t=np.linspace(0.0,0.5,n)  # centered
    for amp in (0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
       starts.append(amp*t)
       starts.append(-amp*t)
    for amp in (1.0,2.0,4.0):
       starts.append(amp*(4*t*t - t)) #?
    for k in (1,2,3,4,5):
       ph=2*np.pi*k*np.arange(n)/n
       for amp in (1.0,2.0,4.0):
          starts.append(amp*np.sin(ph))
          starts.append(amp*np.cos(ph))
    for c in (0.7,1.0,1.7,2.5):
       x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
       starts.append(2*np.sin(2*np.pi*c*x*x))
       starts.append(-2*np.sin(...))
    for sd in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
       z=rng.standard_normal(n)*sd; z-=z.mean(); starts.append(z)
    # random signs smoothed
    u=rng.standard_normal(n); u-=u.mean(); starts.append(np.clip(u,-1,1)*2)
    # random lowpass: Fourier coeffs random
    K=min(8,n//2); phi=rng.normal(size=2K); theta=zeros; for k...; starts.append(theta)
    # trim to max_starts but ensure diversity: take evenly
    if len(starts)>max_starts:
       idxs=np.linspace(0,len(starts)-1,max_starts,dtype=int)
       starts=[starts[i] for i in idxs]
    return starts
```

LP refine top-level:
```
def _lp_refine_sequence(a, deadline, max_iters=5):
    n=len(a)
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
    s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
    a/=s
    best_score=_true_score(a); best=a.copy()
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic()>=deadline: break
       b=_conv_square(a); M=float(np.max(np.maximum(b,0)))
       try:
          g=_lp_direction(a, M, deadline)
       except Exception:
          break
       if g is None: break
       g=np.asarray(g,float).clip(0,1000)
       Sg=g.sum()
       if Sg<=1e-12 or not finite: break
       g/=Sg
       local_best=best; local_score=best_score
       for tval in (0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
           anew=(1.0-tval)*best + tval*g
           anew=np.clip(anew,0,1000)
           sc=_true_score(anew)
           if sc<local_score:
              local_score=sc; local_best=anew.copy()
       if local_score < best_score:
           best=local_best; best_score=local_score
       else:
           break
    return best
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative coefficients minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

The program searches over several sequence lengths.  For each length it
optimizes over theta parameters where a = softmax(theta).  The smoothed
peak is approximated by a log-sum-exp of the convolution coefficients, and
L-BFGS-B is used with an analytic gradient.  Low-dimensional Fourier bases
are also optimized to provide robust non-symmetric starts.  A small dense LP
refinement is used when the length is small enough.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog


def _safe_softmax(theta):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = theta.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    m = np.max(theta)
    e = np.exp(theta - m)
    s = np.sum(e)
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _conv_square(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    A = fft(a, size)
    out = np.real(ifft(A * A, size))[:2 * n - 1]
    return out


def _true_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    b = _conv_square(a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m)


def _theta_score(theta):
    return _true_score(_safe_softmax(theta))


def _lse_value(theta, tau):
    tau = max(1e-12, float(tau))
    a = _safe_softmax(theta)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e300
    b = _conv_square(a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
        return 1e300
    bm = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(bm):
        return 1e300
    z = (b - bm) / tau
    ez = np.exp(z)
    sz = float(np.sum(ez))
    if not np.isfinite(sz) or sz <= 0.0:
        return 1e300
    val = 2.0 * n * (bm + tau * np.log(sz))
    return float(val) if np.isfinite(val) else 1e300


def _lse_grad(theta, tau):
    tau = max(1e-12, float(tau))
    a = _safe_softmax(theta)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    zero = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    b = _conv_square(a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
        return zero

    bm = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(bm):
        return zero

    z = (b - bm) / tau
    ez = np.exp(z)
    sz = float(np.sum(ez))
    if not np.isfinite(sz) or sz <= 0.0:
        return zero
    sw = ez / sz

    swb = float(np.dot(sw, b))
    if not np.isfinite(swb):
        return zero

    # T1[j] = sum_{k=j}^{j+n-1} sw[k] * a[k-j]
    size = next_fast_len(3 * n - 1)
    A = fft(a, size)
    SWR = fft(sw[::-1], size)
    conv = np.real(ifft(A * SWR, size))
    if conv.size < 2 * n - 1:
        return zero
    t1 = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(t1)):
        return zero

    grad = 4.0 * n * a * (t1 - swb)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return zero

    # The parameterization is invariant under adding a constant to theta.
    grad -= float(np.mean(grad))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return zero
    return grad


def _theta_from_coeffs(z, n, K):
    n = int(n)
    K = int(K)
    theta = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if K <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return theta
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        ph = 2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * idx / float(n)
        base = 2 * (k - 1)
        theta += float(z[base]) * np.cos(ph)
        theta += float(z[base + 1]) * np.sin(ph)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return theta


def _coeff_value(z, n, K, tau):
    theta = _theta_from_coeffs(z, n, K)
    return _lse_value(theta, tau)


def _coeff_grad(z, n, K, tau):
    n = int(n)
    K = int(K)
    out = np.zeros(2 * K, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = _theta_from_coeffs(z, n, K)
    gtheta = _lse_grad(theta, tau)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gtheta)):
        return out
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        ph = 2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * idx / float(n)
        base = 2 * (k - 1)
        out[base] = float(np.dot(gtheta, np.cos(ph)))
        out[base + 1] = float(np.dot(gtheta, np.sin(ph)))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(out)):
        return np.zeros(2 * K, dtype=np.float64)
    return out


def _make_options(maxiter, maxfun, maxls=20):
    return {
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": int(maxls),
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-7,
        "disp": False,
    }


def _anneal_theta(theta, n, rng, deadline, iters_per_tau, factors):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = int(n)
    if theta.size != n:
        theta = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    best_theta = theta.copy()
    best_score = _theta_score(theta)

    for factor in factors:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        a = _safe_softmax(theta)
        b = _conv_square(a)
        bmax = float(np.max(np.maximum(b, 0.0))) if b.size else 1.0
        if not np.isfinite(bmax) or bmax <= 0.0:
            bmax = 1.0 / max(1, n)
        tau = max(1e-12, bmax / float(factor))

        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lse_value,
                theta,
                jac=_lse_grad,
                args=(tau,),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options=_make_options(iters_per_tau, 5 * iters_per_tau),
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            cand = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if cand.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
                cs = _theta_score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(cs):
                    if cs < best_score:
                        best_score = cs
                        best_theta = cand.copy()
                        theta = cand
                    elif rng is not None and float(rng.random()) < 0.25:
                        theta = cand

    if _theta_score(best_theta) < best_score:
        best_score = _theta_score(best_theta)
    return best_theta, best_score


def _anneal_coeffs(z, n, K, rng, deadline, iters_per_tau, factors):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = int(n)
    K = int(K)
    if z.size != 2 * K:
        z = np.zeros(2 * K, dtype=np.float64)

    best_z = z.copy()
    best_score = _theta_score(_theta_from_coeffs(z, n, K))

    for factor in factors:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        theta = _theta_from_coeffs(z, n, K)
        a = _safe_softmax(theta)
        b = _conv_square(a)
        bmax = float(np.max(np.maximum(b, 0.0))) if b.size else 1.0
        if not np.isfinite(bmax) or bmax <= 0.0:
            bmax = 1.0 / max(1, n)
        tau = max(1e-12, bmax / float(factor))

        try:
            res = minimize(
                _coeff_value,
                z,
                jac=_coeff_grad,
                args=(n, K, tau),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options=_make_options(iters_per_tau, 5 * iters_per_tau),
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            cand = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if cand.size == 2 * K and np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
                cs = _theta_score(_theta_from_coeffs(cand, n, K))
                if np.isfinite(cs):
                    if cs < best_score:
                        best_score = cs
                        best_z = cand.copy()
                        z = cand
                    elif rng is not None and float(rng.random()) < 0.20:
                        z = cand

    final_score = _theta_score(_theta_from_coeffs(best_z, n, K))
    if final_score < best_score:
        best_score = final_score
    return best_z, best_score


def _initial_theta_starts(n, rng, max_starts=10):
    n = int(n)
    starts = []
    if n <= 0:
        return starts

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    xc = x - 0.5
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    starts.append(np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for amp in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        starts.append(amp * xc)
        starts.append(-amp * xc)

    for amp in (1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        starts.append(amp * (xc * xc - 0.0833))
        starts.append(-amp * (xc * xc - 0.0833))

    for k in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5):
        ph = 2.0 * np.pi * k * idx / n
        for amp in (1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
            starts.append(amp * np.sin(ph))
            starts.append(amp * np.cos(ph))

    for c in (0.7, 1.0, 1.7, 2.5):
        starts.append(2.0 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * c * x * x))
        starts.append(-2.0 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * c * x * x))

    for sd in (0.7, 1.5, 3.0):
        z = rng.standard_normal(n)
        z -= np.mean(z)
        starts.append(sd * z)

    for _ in range(2):
        u = rng.standard_normal(n)
        u -= np.mean(u)
        starts.append(np.clip(2.0 * u, -2.0, 2.0))

    K = min(8, max(2, n // 2))
    for _ in range(2):
        z = rng.standard_normal(2 * K)
        th = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(1, K + 1):
            ph = 2.0 * np.pi * k * idx / n
            th += z[2 * (k - 1)] * np.cos(ph)
            th += z[2 * (k - 1) + 1] * np.sin(ph)
        starts.append(th)

    if len(starts) > max_starts:
        keep = np.linspace(0, len(starts) - 1, max_starts, dtype=int)
        starts = [starts[int(i)] for i in keep]
    return starts


def _lp_direction(a, m, max_n=700):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0 or n > max_n:
        return None
    L = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j + n, j] = a
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bub = np.full(L, float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    res = None
    for method in ("highs", "highs-ds"):
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=bub,
                bounds=bounds,
                method=method,
                options={"disp": False},
            )
            if res is not None and int(res.status) == 0:
                return np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            res = None
    return None


def _lp_refine_sequence(a, deadline, max_iters=5):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    else:
        a = a / s

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _true_score(best)
    n = best.size
    if n > 700:
        return best

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        b = _conv_square(best)
        m = float(np.max(np.maximum(b, 0.0))) if b.size else 1.0
        g = _lp_direction(best, m, max_n=700)
        if g is None:
            break
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, 1000.0)
        gs = float(np.sum(g))
        if not np.isfinite(gs) or gs <= 1e-12:
            break
        g = g / gs

        local_best = best.copy()
        local_score = best_score
        for t in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00):
            cand = (1.0 - t) * best + t * g
            cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
            sc = _true_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_score:
                local_score = sc
                local_best = cand.copy()

        if local_score < best_score:
            best = local_best
            best_score = local_score
        else:
            break

    return best


def _update_best(score, seq, best_score, best_seq):
    if np.isfinite(score):
        seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if seq.size > 0:
            return score, seq.copy()
    return best_score, best_seq


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 15.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240601)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()  # noqa: F821
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
        ps = float(np.sum(parent))
        if np.isfinite(ps) and ps > 0.0:
            pnorm = parent / ps
            sc = _true_score(pnorm)
            best_score, best_seq = _update_best(sc, pnorm, best_score, best_seq)
            print(f"parent sequence score: {best_score:.10f}, n={parent.size}")

    parent_n = int(parent.size) if parent is not None and parent.size > 0 else 0

    lengths = []
    for n in (16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096):
        if 4 <= n <= 8192 and n not in lengths:
            lengths.append(n)
    if 16 <= parent_n <= 8192 and parent_n not in lengths:
        lengths.append(parent_n)
    lengths = sorted(set(lengths))

    factors_short = [10.0, 5.0, 3.0, 2.0, 1.0, 0.7, 0.5]
    factors_long = [10.0, 5.0, 3.0, 2.0, 1.5, 1.0, 0.75, 0.5, 0.35, 0.25, 0.2]

    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 45.0:
            break

        if n == parent_n and n > 512:
            local_budget = 560.0
        elif n >= 2048:
            local_budget = 150.0
        elif n >= 1024:
            local_budget = 120.0
        elif n >= 256:
            local_budget = 90.0
        else:
            local_budget = 35.0
        local_deadline = min(deadline - 5.0, time.monotonic() + local_budget)
        if local_deadline <= time.monotonic() + 15.0:
            continue

        print(f"begining n={n}, budget={local_budget:.1f}s", flush=True)

        # Low-dimensional Fourier optimization.
        K = int(min(max(4, n // 8), 64))
        n_z_tries = 6 if n <= 512 else 4
        z_cands = []
        z_scores = []
        for _ in range(n_z_tries):
            z = rng.standard_normal(2 * K)
            z_cands.append(z)
            z_scores.append(_theta_score(_theta_from_coeffs(z, n, K)))

        z_order = np.argsort(z_scores)
        n_z_opt = 3 if n <= 512 else 2
        for pos in range(min(n_z_opt, z_order.size)):
            if time.monotonic() >= local_deadline:
                break
            idx = int(z_order[pos])
            iters = 260 if n <= 128 else 180 if n <= 256 else 90 if n <= 1024 else 45
            zbest, sc = _anneal_coeffs(
                z_cands[idx], n, K, rng, local_deadline, iters, factors_long
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                seq = _safe_softmax(_theta_from_coeffs(zbest, n, K))
                best_score, best_seq = _update_best(sc, seq, best_score, best_seq)
                if sc < best_score:
                    print(f"  [coeff n={n}] improved {sc:.10f}", flush=True)

        # Full theta optimization from multiple starts.
        raw_starts = _initial_theta_starts(n, rng, max_starts=12 if n <= 512 else 8)

        if n == parent_n and parent is not None and parent.size == n:
            pp = np.clip(parent, 1e-12, 1000.0)
            raw_starts.insert(0, np.log(pp / np.sum(pp)))

        if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size == n:
            bb = np.clip(best_seq, 1e-12, 1000.0)
            raw_starts.insert(0, np.log(bb / np.sum(bb)))

        start_scores = [_theta_score(th) for th in raw_starts]
        for sc, th in zip(start_scores, raw_starts):
            best_score, best_seq = _update_best(sc, _safe_softmax(th), best_score, best_seq)

        order = np.argsort(start_scores)
        n_start_opt = 6 if n <= 256 else 4 if n <= 1024 else 3
        for pos in range(min(n_start_opt, order.size)):
            if time.monotonic() >= local_deadline:
                break
            th0 = raw_starts[int(order[pos])]
            iters = 260 if n <= 128 else 180 if n <= 256 else 90 if n <= 1024 else 40
            thb, sc = _anneal_theta(th0, n, rng, local_deadline, iters, factors_long)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                seq = _safe_softmax(thb)
                best_score, best_seq = _update_best(sc, seq, best_score, best_seq)
                if sc < best_score:
                    print(f"  [theta n={n}] improved {sc:.10f}", flush=True)

        print(f"done n={n}, current best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    if best_seq is None or best_seq.size == 0:
        best_seq = np.ones(16, dtype=np.float64) / 16.0
        best_score = _true_score(best_seq)

    # Optional LP polish for short sequences.
    if best_seq.size <= 700 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        lp_deadline = min(deadline - 10.0, time.monotonic() + 90.0)
        try:
            lp_seq = _lp_refine_sequence(best_seq, lp_deadline, max_iters=6)
            lp_score = _true_score(lp_seq)
            best_score, best_seq = _update_best(lp_score, lp_seq, best_score, best_seq)
            if lp_score < best_score:
                print(f"[LP polish] improved {lp_score:.10f}", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_seq = np.clip(best_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(best_seq))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        best_seq = np.ones(16, dtype=np.float64) / 16.0
        s = 1.0
    # Keep sum equal to 1.  The objective is scale-invariant.
    best_seq = best_seq / s

    try:
        official = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_seq])  # noqa: F821
        if np.isfinite(official) and official < best_score:
            best_score = float(official)
            print(f"[official re-evaluation] {best_score:.10f}", flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"final score estimate: {best_score:.10f}, length={best_seq.size}", flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
